FHDNT ^{
    Build=TEST
    Code ^{
        AddWarehouse17 ~{
            0000=dword nt_storage_planet=0;
            0001=dword nt_storage_hull=0;
            0002=int nt_storage_index=-1;
            0003=if(CT('FHDNT.Build')!='TEST') { exit; }
            0004=if(!Player()) { exit; }
            0005=if(CurrentForm()!='Ship') { exit; }
            0006=if(FormCurShip()!=Player()) { exit; }
            0007=nt_storage_planet=GetShipPlanet(Player());
            0008=if(!nt_storage_planet)
            0009={
            0010=MessageBox('Для выдачи корпуса на склад нужно находиться на планете');
            0011=exit;
            0012=}
            0013=nt_storage_hull=ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.NewHull'),'result');
            0014=if(!nt_storage_hull)
            0015={
            0016=SFT('[FHDNT V18] не удалось создать корпус для склада');
            0017=MessageBox('Не удалось создать Nanotech. Проверьте установку ресурсов мода.');
            0018=exit;
            0019=}
            0020=AddItemToStorage(nt_storage_planet,nt_storage_hull);
            0021=nt_storage_index=FindItemInStorage(nt_storage_hull);
            0022=if(nt_storage_index<0)
            0023={
            0024=FreeItem(nt_storage_hull);
            0025=SFT('[FHDNT V18] корпус не принят складом');
            0026=MessageBox('Не удалось добавить корпус на склад текущей планеты');
            0027=exit;
            0028=}
            0029=InterfaceState('Ship','FHDNT_LevelPicker03',0);
            0030=ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.ModalInput'));
            0031=SFT('Новый Nanotech уровня 0 добавлен на склад текущей планеты');
            0032=UpdateFormShip();
        }
        Apply ~{
            0000=if(ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(h,FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_'+'Mark'))!=1) exit;
            0001=str nt_display_name=CT('FHDNT.HullName');
            0002=int nt_wh_bonus=FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHD_WormholeV2_Core');
            0003=if(nt_wh_bonus>=0)
            0004=if(ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(h,nt_wh_bonus)>0) nt_display_name=nt_display_name+' [WH]';
            0005=SetName(h,nt_display_name);
            0006=ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.EnsureHull'),'h',h);
            0007=ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.Evolve'),'h',h);
            0008=int nt_level=ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(h,FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_'+'Level'));
            0009=if(nt_level<0 || nt_level>20) exit;
            0010=int nt_size=CT('FHDNT.Levels.'+nt_level+'.Size');
            0011=int nt_armor=CT('FHDNT.Levels.'+nt_level+'.Armor');
            0012=int nt_reduction=CT('FHDNT.Levels.'+nt_level+'.Reduction');
            0013=int nt_damage=max(0,ItemSize(h)-HullHP(h));
            0014=if(ItemSize(h)!=nt_size)
            0015={
            0016=ItemSize(h,nt_size);
            0017=HullHP(h,max(1,nt_size-nt_damage));
            0018=}
            0019=ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.Write'),'item',h,'key','Armor','value',nt_armor);
            0020=ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.Write'),'item',h,'key','SlotPenalty','value',0);
            0021=int nt_art_slots=GetEquipmentStats(h,2);
            0022=if(nt_art_slots>4) ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.Write'),'item',h,'key','SlotPenalty','value',nt_art_slots-4);
            0023=int nt_skill=0;
            0024=int nt_slot=0;
            0025=int nt_chosen=0;
            0026=int nt_found=0;
            0027=int nt_resist=0;
            0028=int nt_apply_s1=ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(h,FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_'+'Skill1'));
            0029=int nt_apply_s2=ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(h,FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_'+'Skill2'));
            0030=int nt_apply_s3=ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(h,FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_'+'Skill3'));
            0031=int nt_apply_s4=ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(h,FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_'+'Skill4'));
            0032=for(nt_skill=1;nt_skill<=20;nt_skill=nt_skill+1)
            0033={
            0034=nt_chosen=nt_apply_s1==nt_skill || nt_apply_s2==nt_skill || nt_apply_s3==nt_skill || nt_apply_s4==nt_skill;
            0035=nt_found=FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_Ability'+nt_skill);
            0036=if(nt_chosen && ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(h,nt_found)==0) ItemExtraSpecialsAddByType(h,nt_found,1);
            0037=if(!nt_chosen && ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(h,nt_found)>0) ItemExtraSpecialsDeleteByType(h,nt_found,ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(h,nt_found));
            0038=if(nt_chosen && nt_skill==1) nt_resist=nt_resist+10;
            0039=if(nt_chosen && nt_skill==20) nt_resist=nt_resist+5;
            0040=}
            0041=str nt_axis='';
            0042=int nt_i=0;
            0043=int nt_old=0;
            0044=int nt_new=0;
            0045=int nt_bonus=0;
            0046=for(nt_i=0;nt_i<3;nt_i=nt_i+1)
            0047={
            0048=if(nt_i==0) nt_axis='E';
            0049=if(nt_i==1) nt_axis='S';
            0050=if(nt_i==2) nt_axis='M';
            0051=nt_old=ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(h,FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_'+'Applied'+nt_axis));
            0052=nt_new=max(1,ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(h,FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_'+'Base'+nt_axis))-nt_reduction-nt_resist);
            0053=if(nt_old!=nt_new)
            0054={
            0055=if(nt_old>0)
            0056={
            0057=nt_bonus=FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_R'+nt_axis+'_'+nt_old);
            0058=if(nt_bonus>=0 && ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(h,nt_bonus)>0)
            0059=ItemExtraSpecialsDeleteByType(h,nt_bonus,1);
            0060=}
            0061=nt_bonus=FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_R'+nt_axis+'_'+nt_new);
            0062=if(nt_bonus>=0 && ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(h,nt_bonus)==0)
            0063=ItemExtraSpecialsAddByType(h,nt_bonus,1);
            0064=ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.Write'),'item',h,'key','Applied'+nt_axis,'value',nt_new);
            0065=}
            0066=}
            0067=if(h==ShipItems(Player(),0)) ShipCalcParam(Player());
        }
        Bootstrap ~{
            0000=int nt_supply=ShipFindCustomShipInfoByType(Player(),'FHDNT_Supply042');
            0001=if(nt_supply<0) { ShipAddCustomShipInfo(Player(),'FHDNT_Supply042',''); nt_supply=ShipFindCustomShipInfoByType(Player(),'FHDNT_Supply042'); }
            0002=ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.SectorSupply'));
            0003=ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.TalentInfo'));

        CacheKillRanks23 ~{
            0000=dword nt_cache_hull=0;
            0001=dword nt_cache_star=0;
            0002=dword nt_cache_ship=0;
            0003=int nt_cache_info=-1;
            0004=int nt_cache_i=0;
            0005=int nt_cache_kind=-1;
            0006=int nt_cache_rank=0;
            0007=str nt_cache_scope='';
            0008=str nt_cache_list='';
            0009=if(!Player()) { exit; }
            0010=nt_cache_hull=ShipItems(Player(),0);
            0011=if(!nt_cache_hull) { exit; }
            0012=if(ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(nt_cache_hull,FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_Mark'))!=1) { exit; }
            0013=nt_cache_star=ShipStar(Player());
            0014=if(!nt_cache_star) { exit; }
            0015=nt_cache_info=ShipFindCustomShipInfoByType(Player(),'FHDNT_XP23');
            0016=if(nt_cache_info<0)
            0017={
            0018=ShipAddCustomShipInfo(Player(),'FHDNT_XP23','NoShow');
            0019=nt_cache_info=ShipFindCustomShipInfoByType(Player(),'FHDNT_XP23');
            0020=}
            0021=nt_cache_scope=''+CurTurn()+':'+Id(nt_cache_star);
            0022=if(force!=1 && ShipCustomShipInfoTextData(Player(),nt_cache_info,3)==nt_cache_scope) { exit; }
            0023=for(nt_cache_i=0;nt_cache_i<StarShips(nt_cache_star);nt_cache_i=nt_cache_i+1)
            0024={
            0025=nt_cache_ship=StarShips(nt_cache_star,nt_cache_i);
            0026=if(!nt_cache_ship || nt_cache_ship==Player()) { continue; }
            0027=nt_cache_kind=ShipTypeN(nt_cache_ship);
            0028=if(nt_cache_kind!=t_Ranger && nt_cache_kind!=t_Pirate && nt_cache_kind!=t_Warrior && nt_cache_kind!=t_Transport) { continue; }
            0029=if(nt_cache_kind==t_Pirate || ShipType(nt_cache_ship)=='Smuggler')
            0030={ nt_cache_rank=GetShipPirateRank(nt_cache_ship); }
            0031=else { nt_cache_rank=ShipRank(nt_cache_ship); }
            0032=nt_cache_rank=max(0,min(7,nt_cache_rank));
            0033=nt_cache_list=nt_cache_list+'|'+CurTurn()+':'+Id(nt_cache_star)+':'+Id(nt_cache_ship)+':'+nt_cache_rank+'|';
            0034=}
            0035=if(force==1)
            0036={
            0037=ShipCustomShipInfoTextData(Player(),nt_cache_info,1,nt_cache_list+ShipCustomShipInfoTextData(Player(),nt_cache_info,1));
            0038=}
            0039=else
            0040={
            0041=ShipCustomShipInfoTextData(Player(),nt_cache_info,2,nt_cache_list);
            0042=ShipCustomShipInfoTextData(Player(),nt_cache_info,3,nt_cache_scope);
            0043=}
        }
        CaptureKillRank23 ~{
            0000=dword nt_capture_victim=0;
            0001=dword nt_capture_hull=0;
            0002=int nt_capture_kind=-1;
            0003=int nt_capture_rank=0;

            0015=if(nt_capture_kind!=t_Ranger && nt_capture_kind!=t_Pirate && nt_capture_kind!=t_Warrior && nt_capture_kind!=t_Transport) { exit; }
            0016=if(nt_capture_kind==t_Pirate || ShipType(nt_capture_victim)=='Smuggler')
            0017={ nt_capture_rank=GetShipPirateRank(nt_capture_victim); }
            0018=else { nt_capture_rank=ShipRank(nt_capture_victim); }
            0019=nt_capture_rank=max(0,min(7,nt_capture_rank));

            0025=}
            0026=nt_capture_key='|'+CurTurn()+':'+Id(ShipStar(nt_capture_victim))+':'+Id(nt_capture_victim)+':'+nt_capture_rank+'|';
            0027=nt_capture_pending=ShipCustomShipInfoTextData(Player(),nt_capture_info,1);
            0028=if(findsubstr(nt_capture_pending,nt_capture_key)!=0)
            0029={ ShipCustomShipInfoTextData(Player(),nt_capture_info,1,nt_capture_key+nt_capture_pending); }

        ChooseSkill ~{
            0000=if(ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(h,FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_'+'Mark'))!=1 || choice<1 || choice>20) exit;
            0001=int nt_talent_level=ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(h,FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_'+'Level'));
            0002=int nt_talent_allowed=0;
            0003=if(nt_talent_level>=5) nt_talent_allowed=1;
            0004=if(nt_talent_level>=10) nt_talent_allowed=2;
            0005=if(nt_talent_level>=15) nt_talent_allowed=3;
            0006=if(nt_talent_level>=20) nt_talent_allowed=4;
            0007=int nt_talent_slot=0;
            0008=int nt_talent_empty=0;
            0009=int nt_talent_value=0;
            0010=int nt_talent_count=0;
            0011=for(nt_talent_slot=1;nt_talent_slot<=4;nt_talent_slot=nt_talent_slot+1)
            0012={
            0013=nt_talent_value=ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(h,FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_'+'Skill'+nt_talent_slot));
            0014=if(nt_talent_value>0) nt_talent_count=nt_talent_count+1;
            0015=if(nt_talent_value==choice) exit;
            0016=if(nt_talent_slot<=nt_talent_allowed && nt_talent_value==0 && nt_talent_empty==0)
            0017=nt_talent_empty=nt_talent_slot;
            0018=}
            0019=if(nt_talent_empty==0 || nt_talent_count>=nt_talent_allowed) exit;
            0020=ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.Write'),'item',h,'key','Skill'+nt_talent_empty,'value',choice);
            0021=ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.Apply'),'h',h);
            0022=ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.SyncEquipment'));
            0023=InterfaceState('Ship','FHDNT_SkillPicker',1);
            0024=ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.UI'));
            0025=if(CurrentForm()=='Ship' && FormCurShip()==Player())
            0026={
            0027=UpdateFormShip();
            0028=}
        }
        CleanEquipment ~{
            0000=if(!item) exit;
            0001=int nt_old=ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(item,FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_'+'SkillMinApplied'));
            0002=if(nt_old>0) { SetEquipmentStats(item,max(0,GetEquipmentStats(item,1)-nt_old),1); ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.Write'),'item',item,'key','SkillMinApplied','value',0); }
            0003=nt_old=ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(item,FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_'+'SkillFuelApplied'));
            0004=if(nt_old>0) { SetEquipmentStats(item,max(0,GetEquipmentStats(item,0)-nt_old),0); ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.Write'),'item',item,'key','SkillFuelApplied','value',0); }
            0005=nt_old=ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(item,FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_'+'SkillAmmoApplied'));
            0006=if(nt_old>0)
            0007={
            0008=WeaponMaxAmmunition(item,max(0,WeaponMaxAmmunition(item)-nt_old));
            0009=ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.Write'),'item',item,'key','SkillAmmoApplied','value',0);
            0010=}
        }
        Dispatch ~{
            0000=if(!Player()) exit;
            0001=str nt_gate_form=CurrentForm();
            0002=if(nt_gate_form=='' || nt_gate_form=='MainForm' || nt_gate_form=='GameSettings' || nt_gate_form=='GameLoad' || nt_gate_form=='Load' || nt_gate_form=='Introduction' || nt_gate_form=='SelectFace' || nt_gate_form=='ModsManager' || nt_gate_form=='GameMenu') exit;

            0018=exit;
            0019=}
            0020=if(nt_dispatch_action==t_OnEnteringForm || nt_dispatch_action==t_OnReEnteringForm || nt_dispatch_action==t_OnShowingShipInfo)
            0021={

            0024=ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.Stock'));
            0025=ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.UI'));
            0026=exit;

        Evolve ~{
            0000=if(!h) exit;
            0001=ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.EnsureSeries'),'h',h);
            0002=int nt_evo_level=ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(h,FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_Level'));
            0003=if(nt_evo_level<0 || nt_evo_level>20) exit;
            0004=int nt_evo_skin=ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(h,FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_Plan'+nt_evo_level));
            0005=if(nt_evo_skin<1 || nt_evo_skin>CT('FHDNT.Skins.Count')) exit;
            0006=if(CT('FHDNT.Skins.Allowed.'+nt_evo_skin)!=1) exit;
            0007=int nt_evo_bonus=FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_Skin'+nt_evo_skin);
            0008=if(nt_evo_bonus<0) { SFT('[FHDNT V6] invalid skin bonus '+nt_evo_skin); exit; }
            0009=if(nt_evo_skin==ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(h,FindBonusByNameInCfg('FHDNT_Skin')) && HullSpecial(h)==nt_evo_bonus && HullType(h)==9) exit;
            0010=int nt_evo_damage=max(0,ItemSize(h)-HullHP(h));
            0011=SpecialToEquipment(nt_evo_bonus,h);
            0012=if(HullType(h)!=9 || HullSpecial(h)!=nt_evo_bonus) { SFT('[FHDNT V6] acryn failed '+Id(h)); exit; }
            0013=ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.EnsureSeries'),'h',h);
            0014=HullHP(h,max(1,ItemSize(h)-nt_evo_damage));
            0015=ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('FHDNT.Code.Write'),'item',h,'key','Skin','value',nt_evo_skin);

        SyncEquipment ~{
            0000=function FHDNT_ListCount(str nt_list_text)
            0001={
            0002=result=0;
            0003=int nt_list_cursor=0;
            0004=int nt_list_end=0;
            0005=int nt_list_length=count(nt_list_text);
            0006=while(nt_list_cursor<nt_list_length)
            0007={
            0008=nt_list_end=findsubstr(nt_list_text,',',nt_list_cursor);
            0009=if(nt_list_end<0) nt_list_end=nt_list_length;
            0010=if(nt_list_end>nt_list_cursor) result=result+1;
            0011=nt_list_cursor=nt_list_end+1;
            0012=}
            0013=}
            0014=function FHDNT_ListAt(str nt_list_text,int nt_list_index)

            0030=}
            0031=nt_list_cursor=nt_list_end+1;
            0032=}
